Then test on ALL benchmarks to measure:
  1. Does training improve ocrbench? (should: YES)
  2. Does it improve related benchmarks? (transfer learning)
  3. Does it hurt unrelated benchmarks? (catastrophic forgetting)

PHASE 2: BENCHMARK TRAINING - Train on CHARTQA
Then test on ALL benchmarks to measure:
  1. Does training improve chartqa? (should: YES)
  2. Does it improve related benchmarks? (transfer learning)
  3. Does it hurt unrelated benchmarks? (catastrophic forgetting)
